\chapter{Einführung}

Dieser Band stellt die kombinatorischen Werkzeuge bereit, mit denen später
Produkte beliebiger endlicher Länge behandelt werden. Endliche Wörter werden
als spezielle endliche Folgen konstruiert. Volle planare Binärbäume werden
anschließend durch eindeutig zerlegbare Wörter codiert. Insbesondere werden
weder eine Wortstruktur noch eine Baumstruktur axiomatisch vorausgesetzt.
Induktion und Rekursion auf diesen Mengen sind Sätze, die auf der natürlichen
Induktion und dem Dedekindschen Rekursionssatz beruhen.

Die Trennung ist für die folgenden Bände wesentlich: Erst Band~23 führt mit
der Halbgruppe wieder eine axiomatisch definierte algebraische Struktur ein.
Assoziativität wird dort genau einmal im Blockgesetz benutzt; die übrige
Umklammerungsaussage folgt aus den hier konstruierten Werkzeugen.

\chapter{Endliche Wörter}

\section{Wortmenge und Länge}

Ein Wort über \(A\) ist der Funktionsgraph einer nullbasierten endlichen
Folge mit Werten in \(A\). Die folgende Definition ist durch
\(\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)\) gebunden und bezeichnet daher eine Menge.

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Menge der endlichen Wörter]{%
  \FiniteWords{A}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{w\in\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)\bigm|
    \exists n\in\mathbb N\,
      (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)\bigr\}%
}{FiniteWordSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n\dsep\FiniteWords{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Endliche Wörter}{\FiniteWords{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierte endliche Folgen sind endliche Wörter]{%
  n\in\mathbb N\dsep w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A
  \vdash w\in\FiniteWords{A}%
}{FiniteWordFromTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep i\dsep n\dsep w\dsep z}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{w\subseteq\NatSeg{n}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\NatSeg{n}\subseteq\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[5]{z\in w}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,5]{z\in\NatSeg{n}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{3,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,5]{%
    \exists i\in\NatSeg{n}\,\exists a\in A\,z=(i,a)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\times B\eqvdash
      \exists a\in A\,\exists b\in B\,x=(a,b)}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{%
    i\in\NatSeg{n}\land a\in A\land z=(i,a)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{i\in\NatSeg n}{\rAEa{8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{a\in A\land z=(i,a)}{\rAEb{8}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{a\in A}{\rAEa{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[8]{z=(i,a)}{\rAEb{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{i\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\dsep x\in A\vdash x\in B}[thm]{%
      4,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{(i,a)\in\mathbb N\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\dsep b\in B
      \vdash(a,b)\in A\times B}[thm]{13,11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{z\in\mathbb N\times A}{%
    \rIE{12,14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,5]{z\in\mathbb N\times A}{%
    \rEE{7,8,15}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \forall z\,(z\in w\rightarrow z\in\mathbb N\times A)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{5,16}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\subseteq\mathbb N\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
      \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\in\powerset(\mathbb N\times A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{18}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordSetDef}[def]{19,1,2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Eindeutige Länge eines endlichen Wortes]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \exists!n\in\mathbb N\,
    (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)%
}{FiniteWordLengthUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep m\dsep n}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists n\in\mathbb N\,(w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{m\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{m}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[4]{n\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg{m}=\NatSeg{n}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg m\subseteq\NatSeg n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\vdash A\subseteq B}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{\NatSeg n\subseteq\NatSeg m}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A=B\vdash B\subseteq A}[thm]{5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{m\leq n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegLeqSubset}[thm]{3,4,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{n\leq m}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegLeqSubset}[thm]{3,4,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{m=n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoLeqAntisymmetric}[thm]{3,4,8,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \forall m\,\forall n\,\Bigl(
      \bigl(m\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{m}\to A\bigr)
      \land
      \bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)
      \rightarrow m=n\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rUI{\rRI{\rAI{3,4},10}}}}
  \proofstepwidestar{%
    \existsleone n\,\bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land
      w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x)\eqvdash
      \forall y\forall z((P(y)\land P(z))\rightarrow y=z)}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!n\in\mathbb N\,(w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
      \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{2,12}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Länge eines endlichen Wortes]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordLength{w}\coloneqq
  \iota n\in\mathbb N\,
    (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)%
}{FiniteWordLengthDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Wortlänge}{\WordLength{w}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung durch die Wortlänge]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A
  \end{aligned}%
}{FiniteWordTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Natürlichkeit der Wortlänge]{%
    w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N
  }{w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}%
    [FiniteWordLengthNatural]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists!n\,\bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land
        w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthUnique}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}=\iota n\in\mathbb N\,
      (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\land
        w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{\rAEa{4}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung des Wortes]{%
    w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A
  }{w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}%
    [FiniteWordTypingAtLength]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists!n\,\bigl(n\in\mathbb N\land
        w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthUnique}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}=\iota n\in\mathbb N\,
      (w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\land
        w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{\rAEb{4}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Länge aus einer gegebenen Worttypisierung]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep n\in\mathbb N\dsep
  w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A
  \vdash \WordLength{w}=n%
}{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep n\dsep k}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[3]{w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists!k\in\mathbb N\,
      \bigl(w\colon\NatSeg{k}\to A\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthUnique}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\land
    w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
    \rAI{4,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
    n\in\mathbb N\land w\colon\NatSeg{n}\to A}{%
    \rAI{2,3}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{\WordLength{w}=n}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{\exists! x\,P(x),\; P(a),\; P(b)
      \vdash a=b}[thm]{6,7,8}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Leerwort, Buchstabenwörter und Nichtleerwörter}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Leerwort]{%
  \EmptyWord{A}\coloneqq\varnothing%
}{EmptyWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep\EmptyWord{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Leerwort}{\EmptyWord{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Das Leerwort hat Länge null]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;\WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0
  \end{aligned}%
}{EmptyWordLength}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Das Leerwort ist ein endliches Wort]{%
    \EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}
  }{\EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}}[EmptyWordFinite]
    \proofstepwidestar{0\in\mathbb N}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\NatSeg{0}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordDef}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to M}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\colon\NatSeg{0}\to A}{%
      \rIE{2,3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{1,5}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Die Länge ist null]{%
    \WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0
  }{\WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0}[EmptyWordLengthZero]
    \proofstepwidestar{0\in\mathbb N}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\NatSeg{0}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordDef}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\varnothing\colon\varnothing\to M}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\colon\NatSeg{0}\to A}{%
      \rIE{2,3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\EmptyWord{A}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{1,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\WordLength{\EmptyWord{A}}=0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{6,1,5}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Menge der nichtleeren Wörter]{%
  \NonemptyWords{A}\coloneqq
  \FiniteWords{A}\setminus\{\EmptyWord{A}\}%
}{NonemptyWordSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep\FiniteWords{A}\dsep\NonemptyWords{A}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Nichtleere Wörter}{\NonemptyWords{A}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium für nichtleere Wörter]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\eqvdash
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land\WordLength{w}\neq0%
}{NonemptyWordMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwide{\(\vdash\)}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\setminus\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordSetDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land
      w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash
        x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{\rAEb{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\neq\EmptyWord{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{\WordLength{w}=0}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{w\colon\varnothing\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{w=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{f\colon\varnothing\to M\vdash
        f=\varnothing}[thm]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{w=\EmptyWord{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordDef}[def]{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\bot}{\rBI{6,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{\rCI{7,12}}
  \closeproofpartwide

  \proofpartwide{\(\dashv\)}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land\WordLength{w}\neq0}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{\rAEb{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{w=\EmptyWord{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,4]{\WordLength{w}=0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{EmptyWordLengthZero}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,4]{\bot}{\rBI{3,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\neq\EmptyWord{A}}{\rCI{4,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land w\notin\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{\rAI{2,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\setminus\{\EmptyWord{A}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\setminus B\eqvdash
        x\in A\land x\notin B}[thm]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordSetDef}[def]{10}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung nichtleerer Wörter]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;w\in\FiniteWords{A}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A
  \end{aligned}%
}{NonemptyWordTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Endlichkeit]{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash w\in\FiniteWords{A}
  }{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash w\in\FiniteWords{A}}%
    [NonemptyWordFinite]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land\WordLength w\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{2}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Natürlichkeit der Länge]{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N
  }{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}%
    [NonemptyWordLengthNatural]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land\WordLength w\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{3}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Worttypisierung]{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A
  }{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}%
    [NonemptyWordTypingAtLength]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\in\FiniteWords{A}\land\WordLength w\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rAEa{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{3}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Buchstabenwort]{%
  a\in A\vdash
  \LetterWord{a}\coloneqq\{(0,a)\}%
}{LetterWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep\LetterWord{a}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Buchstabenwort}{\LetterWord{a}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Grundgesetze der Buchstabenwörter]{%
  a\in A\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;\LetterWord{a}(0)=a
  \end{aligned}%
}{LetterWordFacts}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
}
\providecommand{\BXXIIProofLetterWordTyping}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar{1\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOneInN}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar{0\in\NatSeg{1}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{(0,a)\in\NatSeg{1}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\dsep b\in B
      \vdash(a,b)\in A\times B}[thm]{3,1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}=\{(0,a)\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\{(0,a)\}\subseteq\NatSeg{1}\times A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash\{a\}\subseteq A}[thm]{4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LetterWord{a}\subseteq\NatSeg{1}\times A}{%
    \rIE{5,6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\forall i\in\NatSeg 1\,i=0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall b\in A\,\bigl((0,b)\in\LetterWord{a}\leftrightarrow b=a\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{1,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \forall i\in\NatSeg{1}\,\exists!b\in A\,
      \bigl((i,b)\in\LetterWord{a}\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]{1,8,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\colon\NatSeg{1}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{UniqueValuedGraphFunction}[thm]{7,10}}}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Nichtleeres Wort]{%
    a\in A\vdash\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}
  }{a\in A\vdash\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}%
    [LetterWordNonempty]
    \BXXIIProofLetterWordTyping
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{2,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{12,2,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{1=\Succ(0)}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoOne}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{0\in\mathbb N}{\FormulaRefAuto{PeanoZero}[ax]}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{\Succ(0)\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccNonzero}[ax]{14}}
    \proofstepwidestar{1\neq0}{\rIE{13,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{12,16}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Länge eins]{%
    a\in A\vdash\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1
  }{a\in A\vdash\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1}%
    [LetterWordLengthOne]
    \BXXIIProofLetterWordTyping
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{2,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{\LetterWord{a}}=1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{12,2,11}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Auswertung an null]{%
    a\in A\vdash\LetterWord{a}(0)=a
  }{a\in A\vdash\LetterWord{a}(0)=a}%
    [LetterWordValueZero]
    \BXXIIProofLetterWordTyping
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      (0,\LetterWord{a}(0))\in\LetterWord{a}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{11,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      (0,\LetterWord{a}(0))\in\{(0,a)\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordDef}[def]{1,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      (0,\LetterWord{a}(0))=(0,a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      0=0\land\LetterWord{a}(0)=a}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)=(c,d)\eqvdash
        a=c\land b=d}[thm]{14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}(0)=a}{%
      \rAEb{15}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Konkatenation}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Mitgliedschaft in verschobenen Bildmengen]{%
  x\in\{r+j\mid j\in J\}\eqvdash
  \exists j\,(j\in J\land x=r+j)%
}{TranslatedImageSetMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{J\dsep j\dsep r\dsep x}
}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Konkatenation endlicher Wörter]{%
  u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordConcat{u}{v}\coloneqq
  u\cup
  \bigl\{(\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\bigm|
    j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\bigr\}%
}{WordConcatenationDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep j}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Wortkonkatenation}{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Blockzerlegung eines Anfangsabschnitts]{%
  r,s\in\mathbb N\vdash
  \NatSeg{r+s}
  =\NatSeg{r}\cup
    \bigl\{r+j\bigm|j\in\NatSeg{s}\bigr\}%
}{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{j\dsep r\dsep s}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideind[Induktionsanfang]{%
    r\in\mathbb N\vdash
    \NatSeg{r+0}=\NatSeg r\cup
      \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{\NatSeg 0=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegZero}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}
      \leftrightarrow
      \exists j\,(j\in\NatSeg 0\land x=r+j)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall x\,x\notin\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x \not\in \varnothing}[thm]{2,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar{\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}=\varnothing}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,x\notin A\vdash A=\varnothing}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r+0=r}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddRightZero}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \NatSeg{r+0}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg 0\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\cup\varnothing=A}[thm]{5,6}}
  \closeproofpartwideind

  \proofpartwideind[Induktionsschritt]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &r,s\in\mathbb N\dsep
        \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
          \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
    \end{aligned}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{r+s\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{r+\Succ(s)=\Succ(r+s)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddSuccRight}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}=\NatSeg{r+s}\cup\{r+s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSucc}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg s\cup\{s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSucc}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
        \leftrightarrow
        \exists j\,(j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\land x=r+j)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}
        \leftrightarrow
        \exists j\,(j\in\NatSeg s\land x=r+j)\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall j\,\Bigl(
        j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}
        \leftrightarrow
        j\in\NatSeg s\lor j\in\{s\}\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\cup B\eqvdash z\in A\lor z\in B}[thm]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall j\,\Bigl(
        j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}
        \leftrightarrow
        j\in\NatSeg s\lor j=s\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
        \leftrightarrow
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\lor x=r+s\Bigr)}{%
      \rLRS{8,9,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}
        \leftrightarrow
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\lor x\in\{r+s\}\Bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\cup B\eqvdash z\in A\lor z\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\bigl(x\in\{r+s\}\leftrightarrow x=r+s\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{a\}\eqvdash x=a}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall x\,\Bigl(
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
        \leftrightarrow
        x\in\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\Bigr)}{%
      \rLRS{12,13,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}
      =\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\forall x\,(x\in A\leftrightarrow x\in B)
        \vdash A=B}[ax]{15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}}{\rIE{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \NatSeg{\Succ(r+s)}
      =\bigl(\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\bigr)
        \cup\{r+s\}}{\rIE{3,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \bigl(\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\bigr)
        \cup\{r+s\}
      =\NatSeg r\cup
        \bigl(\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(A\cup B)\cup C=A\cup(B\cup C)}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg r\cup
        \bigl(\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}\cup\{r+s\}\bigr)
      =\NatSeg r\cup\{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \NatSeg{r+\Succ(s)}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\Succ(s)}\}}{%
      \rChain{17,18,19,20}}
  \closeproofpartwideind

  \proofpartwide{Induktionsschluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{r\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\in\mathbb N}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{r+s}=\NatSeg r\cup
        \{r+j\mid j\in\NatSeg s\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoInductionRule}[ax]{IA,IS,1,2}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Koordinaten der Konkatenation]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)
  \end{aligned}%
}{WordConcatenationTyping}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep i\dsep j}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung]{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A
  }{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A
  }[WordConcatenationTypingPart]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{u}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      u\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      v\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}
      =\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\cup
        \{\WordLength{u}+j\mid
          j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,j\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      \WordLength{u}+j=j+\WordLength{u}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddCommutative}[thm]{3,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      j+\WordLength{u}\notin\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoTailAvoidsInitialSegment}[thm]{3,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      \bigl(\WordLength{u}+j\notin
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\bigr)}{\rIE{9,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j,k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      (j\in\mathbb N\land k\in\mathbb N)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j,k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      \bigl(\WordLength{u}+j=\WordLength{u}+k
        \rightarrow j=k\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddLeftCancellation}[thm]{3,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall p\,\Bigl(p\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\leftrightarrow{}&
        p\in u\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,
        p=(\WordLength u+j,v(j))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      u\subseteq\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\times A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,v(j)\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A
        \vdash F(x)\in B}[thm]{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      (\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))
      \in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\times A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]{7,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \{(\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\mid
        j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}
      \subseteq\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\times A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\subseteq
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\times A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]{14,15,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[20]{%
      q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,20]{%
      q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\lor
      \exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,
        q=\WordLength u+j}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{3,4,7,20}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,20]{%
      \exists a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{5,6,14,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,20]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall a,b\in A\,\Bigl(&
        (q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\land
        (q,b)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\\[-2pt]
      &\rightarrow a=b\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{Funktion}[def]{5,6,11,13,14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,20]{%
      \existsleone a\,\bigl(a\in A\land
        (q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\existsleone x\,P(x)\eqvdash
        \forall y\forall z((P(y)\land P(z))\rightarrow y=z)}[thm]{23}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,20]{%
      \exists!a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{\exists x\,P(x),\; \existsleone x\,P(x)
        \vdash \exists! x\,P(x)}[thm]{22,24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall q\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\,
      \exists!a\in A\,\bigl((q,a)\in\WordConcat{u}{v}\bigr)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{20,25}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{UniqueValuedGraphFunction}[thm]{19,26}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Anfangsblock]{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)
  }{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)}[WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTypingPart}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      u\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{(i,u(i))\in u}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{4,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=u\cup
      \{(\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\mid
        j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{(i,u(i))\in\WordConcat{u}{v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F
        \vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{3,7}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Verschobener Block]{%
    u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)
  }{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\vdash
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)}%
    [WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
        \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTypingPart}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      v\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{(j,v(j))\in v}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        (x,F(x))\in F}[thm]{4,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      (\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))
      \in\{(\WordLength{u}+k,v(k))\mid
        k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \rEI{2,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=u\cup
      \{(\WordLength{u}+k,v(k))\mid
        k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationDef}[def]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      (\WordLength{u}+j,v(j))\in\WordConcat{u}{v}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in B\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{6,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength{u}+j)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep (x,y)\in F
        \vdash F(x)=y}[thm]{3,8}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Länge einer Konkatenation]{%
  u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
    =\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}%
}{WordConcatenationLength}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[2]{v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Kürzung bei festem Konkatenationsschnitt]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
      \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\\[-2pt]
    &\vdash
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\text{(i)}\;u=u'\\[-2pt]
        &\text{(ii)}\;v=v'
      \end{aligned}
  \end{aligned}%
}{WordConcatenationFixedCutCancellation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v\dsep u'\dsep v'\dsep i\dsep j}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Linker Faktor]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
        \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\vdash u=u'
    \end{aligned}
  }{u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
    \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep
    \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\vdash u=u'}%
    [WordConcatenationFixedCutLeft]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      u\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      u'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{u'}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      u'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\to A}{\rIE{2,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(i)=u(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{1,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,7]{\WordConcat{u'}{v'}(i)=u'(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{1,2,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,7]{u(i)=u'(i)}{\rIE{3,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \forall i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength u}\,u(i)=u'(i)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{7,10}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{u=u'}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{4,6,11}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Rechter Faktor]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
        \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\vdash v=v'
    \end{aligned}
  }{u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}\dsep
    \WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}\dsep
    \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}\vdash v=v'}%
    [WordConcatenationFixedCutRight]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v,u',v'\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\WordLength{u}=\WordLength{u'}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}=\WordConcat{u'}{v'}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
        =\WordLength u+\WordLength v}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{\WordConcat{u'}{v'}}
        =\WordLength{u'}+\WordLength{v'}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,3]{%
      \WordLength u+\WordLength v
        =\WordLength{u'}+\WordLength{v'}}{%
      \rIE{3,4,5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \WordLength u+\WordLength v
        =\WordLength u+\WordLength{v'}}{\rIE{2,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength u,\WordLength v,\WordLength{v'}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \WordLength v=\WordLength{v'}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddLeftCancellation}[thm]{8,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      v\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      v'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{v'}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      v'\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\to A}{\rIE{9,11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,13]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{v}(\WordLength u+j)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,13]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v'}}}{\rIE{9,13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,13]{%
      \WordConcat{u'}{v'}(\WordLength{u'}+j)=v'(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,13]{%
      \WordConcat{u'}{v'}(\WordLength u+j)=v'(j)}{\rIE{2,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,13]{v(j)=v'(j)}{\rIE{3,14,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \forall j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\,v(j)=v'(j)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{13,18}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{v=v'}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{10,12,19}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Leerwortgesetze der Konkatenation]{%
  w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash
  \begin{aligned}[t]

  \end{aligned}%

      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{2,1,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
      \WordConcat{\EmptyWord{A}}{w}(i)=w(i)}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat{\EmptyWord{A}}{w}=w}{%

  \proofpartwideindR[Rechtes Leerwortgesetz]{%

      \WordConcat{w}{\EmptyWord{A}}=w
  }{w\in\FiniteWords{A}\vdash

  \proofpartwideindR[Dreifachzerlegung]{%

    &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]

    &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%

      \bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\lor
      \bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat{u}{v}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat{v}{w}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{11,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \NatSeg{\WordLength{v}+\WordLength{w}}
      =\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\cup
      \{\WordLength{v}+h\mid h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordIndexBlockDecomposition}[thm]{6,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[18]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+\WordLength{w}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,18]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
        k=\WordLength{u}+j\bigr)\ \lor{}\\[-2pt]
      &\bigl(\exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
        k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h\bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationThreeBlockDecomposition}[thm]{1,18}}

    \proofstepwidestar[20]{k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\cup
      \{\WordLength{u}+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{20}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}}{\rIE{16,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}}}{\rIE{14,22}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{10,1,23}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)=u(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{1,20}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)=u(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{1,13,20}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)=u(k)}{\rChain{24,25}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,20]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{27,26}}

    \proofstepwidestar[29]{%
      \exists j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\,
      k=\WordLength{u}+j}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[30]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\land
      k=\WordLength{u}+j}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      k\in\{\WordLength{u}+j\mid
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%

    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}}\cup
      \{\WordLength{u}+j\mid j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in B\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{31}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}}{\rIE{16,32}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      k\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}}}{\rIE{14,33}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{10,1,34}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{\WordConcat{u}{v}(k)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}}\cup
      \{\WordLength{v}+h\mid h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in A\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{v}+\WordLength{w}}}{\rIE{17,37}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      j\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{\WordConcat{v}{w}}}}{\rIE{15,38}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)=\WordConcat{v}{w}(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,13,30,39}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{\WordConcat{v}{w}(j)=v(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{1,30}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{v(j)=\WordConcat{v}{w}(j)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{41}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      \WordConcat{v}{w}(j)=\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{40}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,30]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rChain{35,36,42,43}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,29]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rEE{29,30,44}}

    \proofstepwidestar[46]{%
      \exists h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[47]{%
      h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\land
      k=(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+h}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)=w(h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{10,1,14,47}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)=w(h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,47}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{h\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetN}[thm]{7,47}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      (\WordLength u+\WordLength v)+h
      =\WordLength u+(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddAssociative}[thm]{5,6,50}}
    \proofstepwidestar[47]{%
      k=(\WordLength u+\WordLength v)+h}{\rAEb{47}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      k=\WordLength u+(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \rChain{52,51}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordLength v+h\in
      \{\WordLength v+h\mid h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength w}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TranslatedImageSetMembership}[def]{47}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordLength v+h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v}\cup
      \{\WordLength v+h\mid h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength w}\}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{z\in B\vdash z\in A\cup B}[thm]{54}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordLength v+h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength v+\WordLength w}}{%
      \rIE{17,55}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordLength v+h\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{\WordConcat{v}{w}}}}{%
      \rIE{15,56}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)
      =\WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{1,13,53,57}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      w(h)=\WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{49}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordConcat{v}{w}(\WordLength v+h)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{58}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,47]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rChain{48,59,60}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,46]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rEE{46,47,61}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,18]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{%
      \rOE{19,20,28,29,45,46,62}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall k\in
      \NatSeg{(\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v})+\WordLength{w}}\,
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}(k)
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}(k)}{\rUI{\rRI{18,63}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{\WordConcat{u}{v}}{w}
      =\WordConcat{u}{\WordConcat{v}{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{4,64}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Abgeschlossenheit nichtleerer Konkatenationen]{%
  u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}%
}{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{u}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{u}\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \exists k\in\mathbb N\,\WordLength{u}=\Succ(k)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNonzeroIsSucc}[ax]{3,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[7]{%
    k\in\mathbb N\land\WordLength{u}=\Succ(k)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    \WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}
    =\Succ(k+\WordLength{v})}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddSuccLeft}[thm]{4,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{k+\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{4,7}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    \WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}\neq0}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccNonzero}[ax]{8,9}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}\in\mathbb N}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddClosure}[thm]{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\colon
    \NatSeg{\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTypingPart}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordConcat{u}{v}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{11,12}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}
    =\WordLength{u}+\WordLength{v}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationLength}[thm]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    \WordLength{\WordConcat{u}{v}}\neq0}{%
    \rIE{14,10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{13,15}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rEE{6,7,16}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Endzerlegung, Induktion und Rekursion}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Eindeutige Rechtszerlegung eines Nichtleerwortes]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \exists!u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
  \exists!a\in A\,
    \bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\bigr)%
}{NonemptyWordRightDecomposition}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep u\dsep a}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Existenz]{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
  }{%
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}
  }[NonemptyWordRightDecompositionExistence]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordLengthNatural}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\WordLength{w}\neq0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordMembership}[thm]{1}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{\Pred(\WordLength{w})\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoPredNatural}[thm]{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w}=\Succ(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoPredNonzeroValue}[thm]{3,4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}
      \subseteq\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSubsetSucc}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Pred(\WordLength{w})\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegEndpointInSucc}[thm]{5,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordTypingAtLength}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}
      \colon\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}\to A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\vdash
        F\restriction_C\colon C\to B}[thm]{9,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}
      \in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordFromTyping}[thm]{5,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      =\Pred(\WordLength{w})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthFromTyping}[thm]{11,5,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{9,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}\in\FiniteWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordFinite}[thm]{14}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}=1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordLengthOne}[thm]{13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad\colon\NatSeg{
        \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
        +\WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}}\to A
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationTypingPart}[thm]{11,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      +\WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      =\Pred(\WordLength{w})+1}{\rIE{12,16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Pred(\WordLength{w})+1
      =\Succ(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddOneSucc}[thm]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      +\WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      =\Succ(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{\rChain{18,19}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Succ(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))=\WordLength{w}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      +\WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      =\WordLength{w}}{\rChain{20,21}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      \colon\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\to A}{\rIE{22,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{0\in\NatSeg 1}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegOneMembership}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      0\in\NatSeg{
        \WordLength{\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}}}{%
      \rIE{16,24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
        \bigl(\WordLength{
          w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}+0\bigr)
      =\LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}(0)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationShiftedCoordinate}[thm]{11,15,25}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}(0)
      =w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordValueZero}[thm]{13}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
        \bigl(\WordLength{
          w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}+0\bigr)
      =w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}{\rChain{26,27}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FiniteWordLengthNatural}[thm]{11}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}+0
      =\WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoAddRightZero}[thm]{29}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}
      =\Pred(\WordLength{w})}{\rIE{12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}+0
      =\Pred(\WordLength{w})}{\rChain{30,31}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \Pred(\WordLength{w})
      =\WordLength{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}+0}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{32}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
        (\Pred(\WordLength w))
      =w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}{\rIE{33,28}}
    \proofstepwidestar[35]{i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength w}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,35]{%
      i\in\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}\lor
      i=\Pred(\WordLength w)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoNatSegSuccElim}[thm]{5,6,35}}
    \proofstepwidestar[37]{%
      i\in\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,37]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)
      =w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{WordConcatenationInitialCoordinate}[thm]{11,15,37}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,37]{%
      w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{C\subseteq A\dsep x\in C\vdash
        F\restriction_C(x)=F(x)}[thm]{7,37}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,37]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rChain{38,39}}
    \proofstepwidestar[41]{i=\Pred(\WordLength w)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,41]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rIE{41,34}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,35]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength w)}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength w))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rOE{36,37,40,41,42}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall i\in\NatSeg{\WordLength{w}}\,
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}(i)=w(i)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{35,43}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}
      =w}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{23,9,44}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      w=\WordConcat{
        w\restriction_{\NatSeg{\Pred(\WordLength{w})}}}{
        \LetterWord{w(\Pred(\WordLength{w}))}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\vdash b=a}[thm]{45}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \exists u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\exists a\in A\,
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
      \rEI{\rAI{11,\rEI{\rAI{13,46}}}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
    &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
    &\forall u\in\FiniteWords{A}\,\forall v\in\FiniteWords{A}\,
      \forall a\in A\,\forall b\in A\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
    &\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
       w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}\bigr)
      \rightarrow(u=v\land a=b)\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRightDecompositionJointUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{u\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{v\in\FiniteWords{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{b\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land
      w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{\rAEa{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{w=\WordConcat{v}{\LetterWord{b}}}{\rAEb{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%

      &\quad\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]

      \end{aligned}}{%

  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}

      &\quad\bigl(w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]

    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%

  \closeproofpartwide

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Induktion über nichtleere Wörter]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\forall a\in A\,P(\LetterWord{a})\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(P(u)\rightarrow
        P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})\bigr)\\[-2pt]
    &\vdash\forall w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,P(w)
  \end{aligned}%
}{NonemptyWordInduction}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep u\dsep w}
  \DeltaRow{Prädikate}{P}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Rekursion über nichtleere Wörter]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
    &\exists!F\colon\NonemptyWords{A}\to X\,\Bigl(
      \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\qquad\forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)
    \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{NonemptyWordRecursion}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep X\dsep a\dsep u}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{f\dsep s\dsep F}
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Träger des Anfangsgraphen]{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash G_0\in M
  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash G_0\in M}%
    [NonemptyWordRecursionBaseGraphCarrier]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{W\coloneqq\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{M\coloneqq\powerset(W\times X)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      G_0\coloneqq\{p\in W\times X\mid{}&
        \WordLength{\pi_1(p)}=1\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\pi_2(p)=f(\pi_1(p)(0))\}
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \leftrightarrow w\in W\land y\in X\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_1(w,y)=w\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_2(w,y)=y\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad (w,y)\in W\times X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \WordLength{\pi_1(w,y)}=1\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \pi_2(w,y)=f(\pi_1(w,y)(0))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow{}&
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rLRS{6,7,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{G_0\subseteq W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{G_0\in M}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{4,11}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Elementkriterium des Anfangsgraphen]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow
        w\in W\land y\in X\land
        \WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow
      w\in W\land y\in X\land\WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\bigr)}%
    [NonemptyWordRecursionBaseGraphCriterion]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{W\coloneqq\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{M\coloneqq\powerset(W\times X)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      G_0\coloneqq\{p\in W\times X\mid{}&
        \WordLength{\pi_1(p)}=1\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\pi_2(p)=f(\pi_1(p)(0))\}
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \leftrightarrow w\in W\land y\in X\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_1(w,y)=w\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_2(w,y)=y\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad (w,y)\in W\times X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \WordLength{\pi_1(w,y)}=1\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \pi_2(w,y)=f(\pi_1(w,y)(0))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl((w,y)\in G_0\leftrightarrow{}&
        w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\WordLength w=1\land y=f(w(0))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rLRS{6,7,8,9}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Elementkriterium des Übergangsgraphen]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl(
        (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,
        \bigl((u,x)\in G\land
        w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}
  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash
      \forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl(
      (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow w\in W\land y\in X\land
      \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,\exists a\in A\,
      ((u,x)\in G\land w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land y=s(x,a))\bigr)}%
    [NonemptyWordRecursionTransitionCriterion]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{W\coloneqq\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{M\coloneqq\powerset(W\times X)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \leftrightarrow w\in W\land y\in X\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(a,b)\in A\times B\eqvdash
        a\in A\land b\in B}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_1(w,y)=w\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_1(x,y)=x}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \forall w\,\forall y\,\bigl((w,y)\in W\times X
      \rightarrow\pi_2(w,y)=y\bigr)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{(x,y)\in A\times B\vdash
        \pi_2(x,y)=y}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &T(G)\coloneqq{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad\{p\in W\times X\mid{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad \pi_1(p)=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad \pi_2(p)=s(x,a)\bigr)\}
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[9]{G\in M}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,9]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad (w,y)\in W\times X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad \pi_1(w,y)=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad \pi_2(w,y)=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
        x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]{8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,9]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rLRS{5,6,7,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{9,11}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung des Rekursionsoperators]{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash
      \mathcal E\colon M\to M
  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash
      \mathcal E\colon M\to M}%
    [NonemptyWordRecursionOperatorTyping]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{s\colon X\times A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{W\coloneqq\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar{M\coloneqq\powerset(W\times X)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall G\in M\,\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordRecursionTransitionCriterion}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{G\in M}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\forall w\,\forall y\,\Bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad (w,y)\in T(G)\leftrightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad w\in W\land y\in X\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists u\in W\,\exists x\in X\,{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad \exists a\in A\,\bigl({}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad (u,x)\in G\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad w=\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad y=s(x,a)\bigr)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{5}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{T(G)\subseteq W\times X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{A\subseteq B\coloneq
        \forall x\,(x\in A\rightarrow x\in B)}[def]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{T(G)\in M}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{4,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\forall G\in M\,T(G)\in M}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \exists!E\colon M\to M\,\forall G\in M\,E(G)=T(G)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        \mathcal E\coloneqq{}&
        \iota E\colon M\to M\,{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall G\in M\,E(G)=T(G)
      \end{aligned}}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \mathcal E\colon M\to M\land
      \forall G\in M\,\mathcal E(G)=T(G)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{11,12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{\mathcal E\colon M\to M}{\rAEa{13}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Gleichung des Rekursionsoperators]{%

  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep s\colon X\times A\to X\vdash

    [NonemptyWordRecursionSequenceTyping]

        H(0)=G_0\land{}\\[-2pt]

        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal E(H(n))\Bigr)

      \end{aligned}}{%

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &F,F'\colon W\to X\dsep
      \forall a\in A\,
        (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \vdash F=F'
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
    &F,F'\colon W\to X\dsep
      \forall a\in A\,
        (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
    &\forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \vdash F=F'
    \end{aligned}
  }[NonemptyWordRecursionUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{F,F'\colon W\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
      (F(\LetterWord{a})=f(a)\land F'(\LetterWord{a})=f(a))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar{P(w)\coloneqq F(w)=F'(w)}{\rII}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{\forall a\in A\,P(\LetterWord{a})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{u\in W}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{P(u)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      u\in W\rightarrow\forall a\in A\,\Bigl({}&
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rUE{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6]{%
      \forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land

      \rRE{9,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      a\in A\rightarrow\Bigl({}&

      &F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{\rUE{10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6,7]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
      &F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
      &F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)
      \end{aligned}}{\rRE{11,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6,7]{%
      F(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F(u),a)}{\rAEa{12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6,7]{%
      F'(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})=s(F'(u),a)}{\rAEb{12}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6,7,8]{%
      P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=d\dsep P(b,d)\vdash P(a,c)}[thm]{%
        13,14,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6,7]{%
      P(u)\rightarrow P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}})}{\rRI{8,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
      \forall u\in W\,\forall a\in A\,
      (P(u)\rightarrow P(\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}))}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,\rUI{\rRI{7,16}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{\forall w\in W\,P(w)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordInduction}[thm]{5,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{F=F'}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{1,18}}
