    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(n)\colon\TreeStage{A}{n}\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionStageTyping}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{H(0)\colon\TreeStage{A}{0}\to X}{%
      \rRE{4,\rUE{7}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \TreeStage{A}{0}\in
        \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStagePowerSetTyping}[thm]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\TreeClosure{A}{\TreeStage{A}{0}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{9,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(0)}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageSuccessorEquation}[thm]{4,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{\Succ(0)\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[ax]{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,\forall Q\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,
        E(Q)=H(n)(Q)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionStageEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=H(\Succ(0))(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rRE{11,\rUE{\rRE{12,\rUE{13}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(n))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionStageSuccessorEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      H(\Succ(0))=\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(0))}{%
      \rRE{4,\rUE{15}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      H(\Succ(0))(\LeafTree{A}{a})
        =\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(0))(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rIE{16}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \mathcal R_{f,g}(H(0))(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorLeafEquation}[thm]{1,2,6,8,3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rIE{14,17,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{3,19}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung der Stufenvereinigung]{%
    f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\vdash
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))
  }{f\colon A\to X\dsep g\colon X\times X\to X\vdash
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}%
    [TreeRecursionUnionNodeEquation]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{S\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{T\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,4]{%
      \exists n\in\mathbb N\,(S,T\in\TreeStage{A}{n})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeCommonStage}[thm]{\rAI{3,4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      n\in\mathbb N\land S,T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{n\in\mathbb N}{\rAEa{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{S,T\in\TreeStage{A}{n}}{\rAEb{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\TreeStage{A}{n}\subseteq B}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageSubsetTreeSet}[thm]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\TreeStage{A}{n}\in\powerset(B)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\powerset(A)\eqvdash x\subseteq A}[thm]{9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(n)\colon\TreeStage{A}{n}\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionStageTyping}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      H(n)\colon\TreeStage{A}{n}\to X}{%
      \rRE{7,\rUE{11}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      \mathcal R_{f,g}(H(n))(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
        =g(H(n)(S),H(n)(T))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionOperatorNodeEquation}[thm]{%
        1,2,10,12,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,
        H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(n))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionStageSuccessorEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      H(\Succ(n))=\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(n))}{%
      \rRE{7,\rUE{14}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      H(\Succ(n))(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})
        =\mathcal R_{f,g}(H(n))(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rIE{15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      \TreeStage{A}{n}\in
        \powerset(\NonemptyWords{\TreeAlphabet{A}})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStagePowerSetTyping}[thm]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in\TreeClosure{A}{\TreeStage{A}{n}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeGenerationStepDef}[def]{17,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{%
      \NodeTree{A}{S}{T}\in\TreeStage{A}{\Succ(n)}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeStageSuccessorEquation}[thm]{7,18}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\Succ(n)\in\mathbb N}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{PeanoSuccClosure}[ax]{7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall n\in\mathbb N\,\forall Q\in\TreeStage{A}{n}\,
        E(Q)=H(n)(Q)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionStageEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=H(\Succ(n))(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rRE{19,\rUE{\rRE{20,\rUE{21}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{E(S)=H(n)(S)}{%
      \rRE{\rAEa{8},\rUE{\rRE{7,\rUE{21}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,6]{E(T)=H(n)(T)}{%
      \rRE{\rAEb{8},\rUE{\rRE{7,\rUE{21}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,6]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \rIE{22,16,13,23,24}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4]{%
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{\rEE{5,6,25}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3]{%
      \forall T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{4,26}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{3,27}}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Eindeutigkeit]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep
        \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))
        \vdash E_1=E_2
    \end{aligned}
  }[TreeRecursionUniqueness]
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{E_1,E_2\colon B\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,6]{E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{2}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[3,6]{E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,6]{%
      E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep c=b\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{6,9}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[11]{S\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[12]{T\in B}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,11,12]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{4}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5,11,12]{%
      E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rRE{12,\rUE{\rRE{11,\rUE{5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[13]{%
      g(E_1(S),E_1(T))=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))}{%
      \rIE{\rAEa{13},\rAEb{13}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12,13]{%
      E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rIE{14,16,15}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5,11,12]{%
      E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow
        E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})}{%
      \rRI{13,17}}
    \proofstepwidestar[4,5]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,\Bigl(
          E_1(S)=E_2(S)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad E_1(T)=E_2(T)\rightarrow{}\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad\quad=E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \rUI{\rRI{11,\rUI{\rRI{12,18}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[2,3,4,5]{%
      \forall Q\in B\,E_1(Q)=E_2(Q)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeStructuralInduction}[thm]{10,19}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2,3,4,5]{E_1=E_2}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{FunctionExtensionality}[thm]{1,20}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwide{Schluss}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{f\colon A\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[2]{g\colon X\times X\to X}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{E\colon B\to X}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionTyping}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall a\in A\,E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionLeafEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \forall S,T\in B\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=g(E(S),E(T))}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUnionNodeEquation}[thm]{1,2}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &E_1,E_2\colon B\to X\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall a\in A\,
          E_1(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall a\in A\,
          E_2(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,
          E_1(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E_1(S),E_1(T))\dsep{}\\[-2pt]
        &\forall S,T\in B\,
          E_2(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E_2(S),E_2(T))\vdash E_1=E_2
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{TreeRecursionUniqueness}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,2]{%
      \begin{aligned}[t]
        &\exists!E\colon B\to X\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
        &\quad \forall a\in A\,
          E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=f(a)\land{}\\[-2pt]
        &\quad \forall S,T\in B\,
          E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
        &\qquad=g(E(S),E(T))\Bigr)
      \end{aligned}}{%
      \UEI{3,4,5,6}}
  \closeproofpartwide
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\chapter{Blattwörter, Klammerungen und Auswertung}

\section{Blattwort}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Einbuchstabenabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A},\\[-2pt]
    &\forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\coloneqq\LetterWord{a}\bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{LetterWordFunctionDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Einbuchstabenabbildung}{\operatorname{ein}_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,2}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \exists!F\,\Bigl(
      F\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}\land
      \forall a\in A\,F(a)=\LetterWord{a}
    \Bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TypedFunctionDefinitionPrinciple}[thm]{3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Die Konkatenation ist ein binärer Operator auf
nichtleeren Wörtern]{%
  \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)%
}{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep u\dsep v}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{u,v\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{v}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationDef}[def]{2}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Blattwortabbildung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\exists!F\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{F\dsep\operatorname{ein}_A\dsep\frown}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \operatorname{ein}_A\colon A\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\colon
      \NonemptyWords{A}\times\NonemptyWords{A}
      \to\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{2}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{1,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Blattwort eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\operatorname{wort}_A\coloneqq
      \iota F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}(F(S),F(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Blattwort}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen des Blattwortes]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeLeafWordEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\operatorname{wort}_A}
}
\newcommand{\BXXIIProofTreeLeafWordIota}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordExistenceUnique}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{wort}_A\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\iota F\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        F(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        F(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad(F(S),F(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordDef}[def]}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{wort}_A\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to\NonemptyWords{A}\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(\forall a\in A\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{1,2}}%
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung des Blattwortes]{%
    \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}
  }{\operatorname{wort}_A\colon
      \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}%
    [TreeLeafWordTyping]
    \BXXIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rAEa{3}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Blattgleichung]{%
    a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}
  }{a\in A\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}%
    [TreeLeafWordLeafEquation]
    \BXXIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\operatorname{ein}_A(a)}{%
      \rRE{5,\rUE{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{\operatorname{ein}_A(a)=\LetterWord{a}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordFunctionDef}[def]{5}}
    \proofstepwidestar[5]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{6,7}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung]{%
    S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
  }{S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}%
    [TreeLeafWordNodeEquation]
    \BXXIIProofTreeLeafWordIota
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \operatorname{wort}_A\colon
        \BracketTreeSet{A}\to\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rAEa{3}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
        =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
          (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{3}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{4,6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{4,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})}{%
      \rRE{7,\rRE{6,\rUE{\rUE{5}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[]{%
      \mathsf{BinOp}(\NonemptyWords{A},\frown)}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationBinaryOperation}[thm]}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \BinaryOpFunction{\NonemptyWords{A}}{\frown}
        (\TreeLeafWord{A}{S},\TreeLeafWord{A}{T})
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{11,8,9}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6,7]{%
      \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{10,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}

\section{Klammerungen eines Wortes}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Klammerungsmenge eines Nichtleerwortes]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\coloneqq
  \bigl\{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\bigm|
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr\}%
}{WordBracketingSetDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Klammerungsmenge}{\WordBracketings{A}{w}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Elementkriterium der Klammerungsmenge]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\eqvdash
  \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)%
}{WordBracketingMembership}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{w}
      =\{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\mid\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}=w\}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingSetDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    T\in\{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\mid\TreeLeafWord{A}{S}=w\}
      \eqvdash
    \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{x\in\{u\in A\mid P(u)\}\eqvdash
      x\in A\land P(x)}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    T\in\WordBracketings{A}{w}\eqvdash
    \bigl(T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=w\bigr)}{%
    \rIE{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz einer Klammerung]{%
  w\in\NonemptyWords{A}\vdash
  \WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing%
}{WordBracketingExistence}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep w\dsep a\dsep u\dsep T}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafClosure}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordLeafEquation}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \rAI{3,4}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in
      \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{2,5}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{6}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall a\in A\,
      \bigl(\WordBracketings{A}{\LetterWord{a}}\neq\varnothing\bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{1,7}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[9]{u\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{a\in A}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[11]{%
    \exists T\,\bigl(T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}\bigr)}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{A\neq\varnothing\eqvdash
      \exists x\,(x\in A)}[thm]{11}}
  \proofstepwidestar[13]{T\in\WordBracketings{A}{u}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{%
    T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{9,13}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rAEa{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,13]{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}=u}{\rAEb{14}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \LeafTree{A}{a}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafClosure}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeNodeClosure}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\LetterWord{a}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordLeafEquation}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{\TreeLeafWord{A}{T}}
      {\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeLeafWordNodeEquation}[thm]{15,17}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\TreeLeafWord{A}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}}{%
    \rIE{16,20}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
    =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rIE{19,21}}
  \proofstepwidestar[10]{\LetterWord{a}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{LetterWordNonempty}[thm]{10}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordConcatenationClosure}[thm]{9,23}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\land
    \TreeLeafWord{A}{\NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}}
      =\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}{%
    \rAI{18,22}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \NodeTree{A}{T}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}
    \in\WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{WordBracketingMembership}[thm]{24,25}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,13]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{a\in A\vdash A\neq\varnothing}[thm]{26}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10,11]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rEE{12,13,27}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9,10]{%
    \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
    \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
      \neq\varnothing}{%
    \rRI{11,28}}
  \proofstepwidestar[9]{%
    \forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{10,29}}}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{%
    \forall u\in\NonemptyWords{A}\,\forall a\in A\,\Bigl(
      \WordBracketings{A}{u}\neq\varnothing\to
      \WordBracketings{A}{\WordConcat{u}{\LetterWord{a}}}
        \neq\varnothing\Bigr)}{%
    \rUI{\rRI{9,30}}}

  \proofstepwidestar[32]{w\in\NonemptyWords{A}}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[32]{\WordBracketings{A}{w}\neq\varnothing}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{NonemptyWordInduction}[thm]{8,31,32}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\section{Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Existenz und Eindeutigkeit der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep E\dsep\Id_A}
}
\begin{tabproofwide}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[]{\Id_A\colon A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IdA}[thm]}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\colon A\times A\to A}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{FullPlanarBinaryTreeRecursion}[thm]{2,3}}
\end{tabproofwide}

\FormulaDefDeltaK[Auswertung eines Klammerungsbaums]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\coloneqq
      \iota E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall a\in A\,
      E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
    &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
      E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})=
        \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}(E(S),E(T))
      \Bigr)
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationDef}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
  \DeltaRow{\textbf{Neues Symbol}}{}
  \DeltaPrem{Baumauswertung}{\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}

\FormulaThmDeltaK[Typisierung und Rekursionsgleichungen der Baumauswertung]{%
  \begin{aligned}[t]
    &\text{(i)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(ii)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a\\[-2pt]
    &\text{(iii)}\;\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep
      S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}
  \end{aligned}%
}{TreeEvaluationEquations}{%
  \DeltaRow{Mengen}{A\dsep a\dsep S\dsep T}
  \DeltaRow{Funktionen}{\star\dsep\operatorname{ev}_{\star}}
}
\newcommand{\BXXIIProofTreeEvaluationIota}{%
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)}{\rA}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\exists!E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationExistenceUnique}[thm]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{ev}_{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\iota E\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\to A\,\Bigl(\\[-2pt]
      &\forall a\in A\,
        E(\LeafTree{A}{a})=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        E(\NodeTree{A}{S}{T})\\[-2pt]
      &\quad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad(E(S),E(T))
        \Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{TreeEvaluationDef}[def]{1}}
  \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
    \begin{aligned}[t]
      &\operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\Bigl(\forall a\in A\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\Id_A(a)\ \land{}\\[-2pt]
      &\quad \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad=\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}\\[-2pt]
      &\qquad\quad
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})\Bigr)
    \end{aligned}}{%
    \FormulaRefAuto{IotaEvaluation}[thm]{2,3}}%
}
\begin{tabproofsplitwide}
  \proofpartwideindR[Typisierung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\vdash
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}%
    [TreeEvaluationTyping]
    \BXXIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}{\rAEa{4}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Blattgleichung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep a\in A\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a}%
    [TreeEvaluationLeafEquation]
    \BXXIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall a\in A\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
      \rAEa{\rAEb{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{a\in A}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=\Id_A(a)}{%
      \rRE{6,\rUE{5}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[6]{\Id_A(a)=a}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{x\in A\vdash\Id_A(x)=x}[thm]{6}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,6]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\LeafTree{A}{a}}=a}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{7,8}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR

  \proofpartwideindR[Knotengleichung der Baumauswertung]{%
    \mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}
  }{\mathsf{BinOp}(A,\star)\dsep S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\vdash
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}%
    [TreeEvaluationNodeEquation]
    \BXXIIProofTreeEvaluationIota
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \operatorname{ev}_{\star}\colon\BracketTreeSet{A}\to A}{\rAEa{4}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1]{%
      \forall S,T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}\,
        \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
        =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
          (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
      \rAEb{\rAEb{4}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[7]{S\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[8]{T\in\BracketTreeSet{A}}{\rA}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{5,7}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,8]{\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}\in A}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{F\colon A\to B\dsep x\in A\vdash
        F(x)\in B}[thm]{5,8}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})}{%
      \rRE{8,\rRE{7,\rUE{\rUE{6}}}}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \BinaryOpFunction{A}{\star}
        (\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S},\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T})
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{BinaryOperationFunctionDef}[def]{1,9,10}}
    \proofstepwidestar[1,7,8]{%
      \TreeEvaluation{\star}{\NodeTree{A}{S}{T}}
      =\TreeEvaluation{\star}{S}\star\TreeEvaluation{\star}{T}}{%
      \FormulaRefAuto{a=b\dsep b=c\vdash a=c}[thm]{11,12}}
  \closeproofpartwideindR
\end{tabproofsplitwide}
